style=\"max-width: 100%;\"\u003e\u003c/a\u003e\n\u003cul dir=\"auto\"\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003ca href=\"https://langfuse.com/docs/tracing\" rel=\"nofollow\"\u003eLLM Application Observability\u003c/a\u003e: Instrument your app and start ingesting traces to Langfuse, thereby tracking LLM calls and other relevant logic in your app such as retrieval, embedding, or agent actions. Inspect and debug complex logs and user sessions. Try the interactive \u003ca href=\"https://langfuse.com/docs/demo\" rel=\"nofollow\"\u003edemo\u003c/a\u003e to see this in action.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003ca href=\"https://langfuse.com/docs/prompt-management/get-started\" rel=\"nofollow\"\u003ePrompt Management\u003c/a\u003e helps you centrally manage, version control, and collaboratively iterate on your prompts. Thanks to strong caching on server and client side, you can iterate on prompts without adding latency to your application.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003ca href=\"https://langfuse.com/docs/evaluation/overview\" rel=\"nofollow\"\u003eEvaluations\u003c/a\u003e are key to the LLM application development workflow, and Langfuse adapts to your needs. It supports LLM-as-a-judge, Code evaluators, user feedback collection, manual labeling, and custom evaluation pipelines via APIs/SDKs.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003ca href=\"https://langfuse.com/docs/evaluation/dataset-runs/datasets\" rel=\"nofollow\"\u003eDatasets\u003c/a\u003e enable test sets and benchmarks for evaluating your LLM application. They support continuous improvement, pre-deployment testing, structured experiments, flexible evaluation, and seamless integration with frameworks like LangChain and LlamaIndex.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003ca href=\"https://langfuse.com/docs/playground\" rel=\"nofollow\"\u003eLLM Playground\u003c/a\u003e is a tool for testing and iterating on your prompts and model configurations, shortening the feedback loop and accelerating development. When you see a bad result in tracing, you can directly jump to the playground to iterate on it.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003ca href=\"https://langfuse.com/docs/api\" rel=\"nofollow\"\u003eComprehensive API\u003c/a\u003e: Langfuse is frequently used to power bespoke LLMOps workflows while using the building blocks provided by Langfuse via the API. OpenAPI spec, Postman collection, and typed SDKs for Python, JS/TS are available.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003c/ul\u003e\n\u003cdiv class=\"markdown-heading\" dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003ch2 </p>
<p dir="auto">Langfuse is an <strong>open source LLM engineering</strong> platform. It helps teams collaboratively
<strong>develop, monitor, evaluate,</strong> and <strong>debug</strong> AI applications. Langfuse can be <strong>self-hosted in minutes</strong> and is <strong>battle-tested</strong>. Proudly made with <a href="https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse">ClickHouse open source database</a>.</p>
<blockquote>

<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h2 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">✨ Core Features</h2><a id="user-content--core-features" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: ✨ Core Features" href="#-core-features"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<a target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" href="https://private-user-images.githubusercontent.com/48529566/600965826-0fad3dee-f3ad-423c-9f0d-7ccd0f26cc2d.png?jwt=eyJ0eXAiOiJKV1QiLCJhbGciOiJIUzI1NiJ9.eyJpc3MiOiJnaXRodWIuY29tIiwiYXVkIjoicmF3LmdpdGh1YnVzZXJjb250ZW50LmNvbSIsImtleSI6ImtleTUiLCJleHAiOjE3ODUzMDYwMzAsIm5iZiI6MTc4NTMwNTczMCwicGF0aCI6Ii80ODUyOTU2Ni82MDA5NjU4MjYtMGZhZDNkZWUtZjNhZC00MjNjLTlmMGQtN2NjZDBmMjZjYzJkLnBuZz9YLUFtei1BbGdvcml0aG09QVdTNC1ITUFDLVNIQTI1NiZYLUFtei1DcmVkZW50aWFsPUFLSUFWQ09EWUxTQTUzUFFLNFpBJTJGMjAyNjA3MjklMkZ1cy1lYXN0LTElMkZzMyUyRmF3czRfcmVxdWVzdCZYLUFtei1EYXRlPTIwMjYwNzI5VDA2MTUzMFomWC1BbXotRXhwaXJlcz0zMDAmWC1BbXotU2lnbmF0dXJlPTAzMzI4MDA2NmI1YTM3ODYzNzVkMzUyMjYzMTQwMzJjNWIzYWU3ZGE0OWVlYmZmMGFhMTZiOWQyM2IwMTY3ZjYmWC1BbXotU2lnbmVkSGVhZGVycz1ob3N0JnJlc3BvbnNlLWNvbnRlbnQtdHlwZT1pbWFnZSUyRnBuZyJ9.mshMqUIwrcGmasVVEVkXLaUCCsR2x93SEkqh-DRLr6Y"><img width="2000" alt="features" src="https://private-user-images.githubusercontent.com/48529566/600965826-0fad3dee-f3ad-423c-9f0d-7ccd0f26cc2d.png?jwt=eyJ0eXAiOiJKV1QiLCJhbGciOiJIUzI1NiJ9.eyJpc3MiOiJnaXRodWIuY29tIiwiYXVkIjoicmF3LmdpdGh1YnVzZXJjb250ZW50LmNvbSIsImtleSI6ImtleTUiLCJleHAiOjE3ODUzMDYwMzAsIm5iZiI6MTc4NTMwNTczMCwicGF0aCI6Ii80ODUyOTU2Ni82MDA5NjU4MjYtMGZhZDNkZWUtZjNhZC00MjNjLTlmMGQtN2NjZDBmMjZjYzJkLnBuZz9YLUFtei1BbGdvcml0aG09QVdTNC1ITUFDLVNIQTI1NiZYLUFtei1DcmVkZW50aWFsPUFLSUFWQ09EWUxTQTUzUFFLNFpBJTJGMjAyNjA3MjklMkZ1cy1lYXN0LTElMkZzMyUyRmF3czRfcmVxdWVzdCZYLUFtei1EYXRlPTIwMjYwNzI5VDA2MTUzMFomWC1BbXotRXhwaXJlcz0zMDAmWC1BbXotU2lnbmF0dXJlPTAzMzI4MDA2NmI1YTM3ODYzNzVkMzUyMjYzMTQwMzJjNWIzYWU3ZGE0OWVlYmZmMGFhMTZiOWQyM2IwMTY3ZjYmWC1BbXotU2lnbmVkSGVhZGVycz1ob3N0JnJlc3BvbnNlLWNvbnRlbnQtdHlwZT1pbWFnZSUyRnBuZyJ9.mshMqUIwrcGmasVVEVkXLaUCCsR2x93SEkqh-DRLr6Y" style="max-width: 100%;"></a>
<ul dir="auto">
<li>
<p dir="auto"><a href="https://langfuse.com/docs/tracing" rel="nofollow">LLM Application Observability</a>: Instrument your app and start ingesting traces to Langfuse, thereby tracking LLM calls and other relevant logic in your app such as retrieval, embedding, or agent actions. Inspect and debug complex logs and user sessions. Try the interactive <a href="https://langfuse.com/docs/demo" rel="nofollow">demo</a> to see this in action.</p>
</li>
<li>
<p dir="auto"><a href="https://langfuse.com/docs/prompt-management/get-started" rel="nofollow">Prompt Management</a> helps you centrally manage, version control, and collaboratively iterate on your prompts. Thanks to strong caching on server and client side, you can iterate on prompts without adding latency to your application.</p>
</li>
<li>
<p dir="auto"><a href="https://langfuse.com/docs/evaluation/overview" rel="nofollow">Evaluations</a> are key to the LLM application development workflow, and Langfuse adapts to your needs. It supports LLM-as-a-judge, Code evaluators, user feedback collection, manual labeling, and custom evaluation pipelines via APIs/SDKs.</p>
</li>
<li>
<p dir="auto"><a href="https://langfuse.com/docs/evaluation/dataset-runs/datasets" rel="nofollow">Datasets</a> enable test sets and benchmarks for evaluating your LLM application. They support continuous improvement, pre-deployment testing, structured experiments, flexible evaluation, and seamless integration with frameworks like LangChain and LlamaIndex.</p>
</li>
<li>
<p dir="auto"><a href="https://langfuse.com/docs/playground" rel="nofollow">LLM Playground</a> is a tool for testing and iterating on your prompts and model configurations, shortening the feedback loop and accelerating development. When you see a bad result in tracing, you can directly jump to the playground to iterate on it.</p>
</li>
<li>
<p dir="auto"><a href="https://langfuse.com/docs/api" rel="nofollow">Comprehensive API</a>: Langfuse is frequently used to power bespoke LLMOps workflows while using the building blocks provided by Langfuse via the API. OpenAPI spec, Postman collection, and typed SDKs for Python, JS/TS are available.</p>
</li>
</ul>

<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h3 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Self-Host Langfuse</h3><a id="user-content-self-host-langfuse" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Self-Host Langfuse" href="#self-host-langfuse"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">Run Langfuse on your own infrastructure:</p>
<ul dir="auto">
<li>
<p dir="auto"><a href="https://langfuse.com/self-hosting/local" rel="nofollow">Local (docker compose)</a>: Run Langfuse on your own machine in 5 minutes using Docker Compose.</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-shell notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="# Get a copy of the latest Langfuse repository
git clone --depth=1 https://github.com/langfuse/langfuse.git
cd langfuse

# Run the langfuse docker compose
docker compose up"><pre><span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span> Get a copy of the latest Langfuse repository</span>
git clone --depth=1 https://github.com/langfuse/langfuse.git
<span class="pl-c1">cd</span> langfuse

<span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span> Run the langfuse docker compose</span>
docker compose up</pre></div>
</li>
<li>
<p dir="auto"><a href="https://langfuse.com/self-hosting/docker-compose" rel="nofollow">VM</a>: Run Langfuse on a single Virtual Machine using Docker Compose.</p>
</li>
<li>
<p dir="auto"><a href="https://langfuse.com/self-hosting/kubernetes-helm" rel="nofollow">Kubernetes (Helm)</a>: Run Langfuse on a Kubernetes cluster using Helm. This is the preferred production deployment.</p>
</li>
<li>
<p dir="auto">Terraform Templates: <a href="https://langfuse.com/self-hosting/aws" rel="nofollow">AWS</a>, <a href="https://langfuse.com/self-hosting/azure" rel="nofollow">Azure</a>, <a href="https://langfuse.com/self-hosting/gcp" rel="nofollow">GCP</a></p>
</li>
</ul>
<p dir="auto">See <a href="https://langfuse.com/self-hosting" rel="nofollow">self-hosting documentation</a> to learn more about architecture and configuration options.</p>

</div>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h2 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">🔌 Integrations</h2><a id="user-content--integrations" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: 🔌 Integrations" href="#-integrations"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>

<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h2 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">🚀 Quickstart</h2><a id="user-content--quickstart" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: 🚀 Quickstart" href="#-quickstart"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">Instrument your app and start ingesting traces to Langfuse, thereby tracking LLM calls and other relevant logic in your app such as retrieval, embedding, or agent actions. Inspect and debug complex logs and user sessions.</p>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h3 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">1️⃣ Create new project</h3><a id="user-content-1️⃣-create-new-project" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: 1️⃣ Create new project" href="#1️⃣-create-new-project"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<ol dir="auto">
<li><a href="https://cloud.langfuse.com/auth/sign-up" rel="nofollow">Create Langfuse account</a> or <a href="https://langfuse.com/self-hosting" rel="nofollow">self-host</a></li>
<li>Create a new project</li>
<li>Create new API credentials in the project settings</li>
</ol>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h3 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">2️⃣ Log your first LLM call</h3><a id="user-content-2️⃣-log-your-first-llm-call" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: 2️⃣ Log your first LLM call" href="#2️⃣-log-your-first-llm-call"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">The <a href="https://langfuse.com/docs/sdk/python/decorators" rel="nofollow"><code>@observe()</code> decorator</a> makes it easy to trace any Python LLM application. In this quickstart we also use the Langfuse <a href="https://langfuse.com/integrations/model-providers/openai-py" rel="nofollow">OpenAI integration</a> to automatically capture all model parameters.</p>
<div class="markdown-alert markdown-alert-tip" dir="auto"><p class="markdown-alert-title" dir="auto"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-light-bulb mr-2" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="M8 1.5c-2.363 0-4 1.69-4 3.75 0 .984.424 1.625.984 2.304l.214.253c.223.264.47.556.673.848.284.411.537.896.621 1.49a.75.75 0 0 1-1.484.211c-.04-.282-.163-.547-.37-.847a8.456 8.456 0 0 0-.542-.68c-.084-.1-.173-.205-.268-.32C3.201 7.75 2.5 6.766 2.5 5.25 2.5 2.31 4.863 0 8 0s5.5 2.31 5.5 5.25c0 1.516-.701 2.5-1.328 3.259-.095.115-.184.22-.268.319-.207.245-.383.453-.541.681-.208.3-.33.565-.37.847a.751.751 0 0 1-1.485-.212c.084-.593.337-1.078.621-1.489.203-.292.45-.584.673-.848.075-.088.147-.173.213-.253.561-.679.985-1.32.985-2.304 0-2.06-1.637-3.75-4-3.75ZM5.75 12h4.5a.75.75 0 0 1 0 1.5h-4.5a.75.75 0 0 1 0-1.5ZM6 15.25a.75.75 0 0 1 .75-.75h2.5a.75.75 0 0 1 0 1.5h-2.5a.75.75 0 0 1-.75-.75Z"></path></svg>Tip</p><p dir="auto">Not using OpenAI? Visit <a href="https://langfuse.com/docs/get-started#log-your-first-llm-call-to-langfuse" rel="nofollow">our documentation</a> to learn how to log other models and frameworks.</p>
</div>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-shell notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="pip install langfuse openai"><pre>pip install langfuse openai</pre></div>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-shell notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="LANGFUSE_SECRET_KEY=&quot;sk-lf-...&quot;
LANGFUSE_PUBLIC_KEY=&quot;pk-lf-...&quot;
LANGFUSE_BASE_URL=&quot;https://cloud.langfuse.com&quot; # 🇪🇺 EU region
# LANGFUSE_BASE_URL=&quot;https://us.cloud.langfuse.com&quot; # 🇺🇸 US region"><pre>LANGFUSE_SECRET_KEY=<span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">"</span>sk-lf-...<span class="pl-pds">"</span></span>
LANGFUSE_PUBLIC_KEY=<span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">"</span>pk-lf-...<span class="pl-pds">"</span></span>
LANGFUSE_BASE_URL=<span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">"</span>https://cloud.langfuse.com<span class="pl-pds">"</span></span> <span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span> 🇪🇺 EU region</span>
<span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span> LANGFUSE_BASE_URL="https://us.cloud.langfuse.com" # 🇺🇸 US region</span></pre></div>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-python notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="from langfuse import observe
from langfuse.openai import openai # OpenAI integration

@observe()
def story():
    return openai.chat.completions.create(
        model=&quot;gpt-4o&quot;,
        messages=[{&quot;role&quot;: &quot;user&quot;, &quot;content&quot;: &quot;What is Langfuse?&quot;}],
    ).choices[0].message.content

@observe()
def main():
    return story()

main()"><pre><span class="pl-k">from</span> <span class="pl-s1">langfuse</span> <span class="pl-k">import</span> <span class="pl-s1">observe</span>
<span class="pl-k">from</span> <span class="pl-s1">langfuse</span>.<span class="pl-s1">openai</span> <span class="pl-k">import</span> <span class="pl-s1">openai</span> <span class="pl-c"># OpenAI integration</span>

<span class="pl-en">@<span class="pl-en">observe</span>()</span>
<span class="pl-k">def</span> <span class="pl-en">story</span>():
    <span class="pl-k">return</span> <span class="pl-s1">openai</span>.<span class="pl-c1">chat</span>.<span class="pl-c1">completions</span>.<span class="pl-c1">create</span>(
        <span class="pl-s1">model</span><span class="pl-c1">=</span><span class="pl-s">"gpt-4o"</span>,
        <span class="pl-s1">messages</span><span class="pl-c1">=</span>[{<span class="pl-s">"role"</span>: <span class="pl-s">"user"</span>, <span class="pl-s">"content"</span>: <span class="pl-s">"What is Langfuse?"</span>}],
    ).<span class="pl-c1">choices</span>[<span class="pl-c1">0</span>].<span class="pl-c1">message</span>.<span class="pl-c1">content</span>

<span class="pl-en">@<span class="pl-en">observe</span>()</span>
<span class="pl-k">def</span> <span class="pl-en">main</span>():
    <span class="pl-k">return</span> <span class="pl-en">story</span>()

<span class="pl-en">main</span>()</pre></div>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h3 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">3️⃣ See traces in Langfuse</h3><a id="user-content-3️⃣-see-traces-in-langfuse" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: 3️⃣ See traces in Langfuse" href="#3️⃣-see-traces-in-langfuse"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>

<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h2 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">💭 Support</h2><a id="user-content--support" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: 💭 Support" href="#-support"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">Finding an answer to your question:</p>
<ul dir="auto">
<li>Our <a href="https://langfuse.com/docs" rel="nofollow">documentation</a> is the best place to start looking for answers. It is comprehensive, and we invest significant time into maintaining it. You can also suggest edits to the docs via GitHub.</li>
<li><a href="https://langfuse.com/faq" rel="nofollow">Langfuse FAQs</a> where the most common questions are answered.</li>
<li>Use "<a href="https://langfuse.com/docs/ask-ai" rel="nofollow">Ask AI</a>" to get instant answers to your questions.</li>
</ul>
<p dir="auto">Support Channels:</p>
<ul dir="auto">
<li><strong>Ask any question in our <a href="https://github.com/orgs/langfuse/discussions/categories/support">public Q&amp;A</a> on GitHub Discussions.</strong> Please include as much detail as possible (e.g. code snippets, screenshots, background information) to help us understand your question.</li>
<li><a href="https://github.com/orgs/langfuse/discussions/categories/ideas">Request a feature</a> on GitHub Discussions.</li>
<li><a href="https://github.com/langfuse/langfuse/issues">Report a Bug</a> on GitHub Issues.</li>
<li>For time-sensitive queries, ping us via the in-app chat widget.</li>
</ul>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h2 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">🤝 Contributing</h2><a id="user-content--contributing" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: 🤝 Contributing" href="#-contributing"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">Your contributions are welcome!</p>
<ul dir="auto">
<li>Vote on <a href="https://github.com/orgs/langfuse/discussions/categories/ideas">Ideas</a> in GitHub Discussions.</li>

</ul>

<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h2 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Dependencies</h2><a id="user-content-dependencies" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Dependencies" href="#dependencies"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">We deploy this code base in Docker containers based on the Linux Alpine Image (<a href="https://github.com/nodejs/docker-node">source</a>). You may find the Dockerfiles in <a href="/langfuse/langfuse/blob/main/web/Dockerfile">web/Dockerfile</a> and <a href="/langfuse/langfuse/blob/main/worker/Dockerfile">worker/Dockerfile</a>.</p>

<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h2 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">🔒 Security &amp; Privacy</h2><a id="user-content--security--privacy" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: 🔒 Security &amp; Privacy" href="#-security--privacy"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">We take data security and privacy seriously. Please refer to our <a href="https://langfuse.com/security" rel="nofollow">Security and Privacy</a> page for more information.</p>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h3 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Telemetry</h3><a id="user-content-telemetry" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Telemetry" href="#telemetry"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">By default, Langfuse automatically reports basic usage statistics of self-hosted instances to a centralized server (PostHog).</p>
<p dir="auto">This helps us to:</p>
<ol dir="auto">
<li>Understand how Langfuse is used and improve the most relevant features.</li>
<li>Track overall usage for internal and external (e.g. fundraising) reporting.</li>
</ol>
<p dir="auto">The telemetry does not include raw traces, prompts, observations, scores, or dataset contents. We document the exact fields that are collected, where they are sent, and the implementation reference in our <a href="https://langfuse.com/self-hosting/security/telemetry" rel="nofollow">telemetry docs</a>.</p>
<p dir="auto">For Langfuse OSS, you can opt out by setting <code>TELEMETRY_ENABLED=false</code>.</p>

<p dir="auto"><a target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer nofollow" href="https://camo.githubusercontent.com/f1ac4a8e7f0d4469dbfc6e28c9e9b8a73f9f84a967c378c555761e6da32d6b26/68747470733a2f2f7374617469632e73636172662e73682f612e706e673f782d707869643d35663962373030662d656539342d343934302d623030352d65373264656630303866666126706167653d524541444d452e6d64"><img src="https://camo.githubusercontent.com/f1ac4a8e7f0d4469dbfc6e28c9e9b8a73f9f84a967c378c555761e6da32d6b26/68747470733a2f2f7374617469632e73636172662e73682f612e706e673f782d707869643d35663962373030662d656539342d343934302d623030352d65373264656630303866666126706167653d524541444d452e6d64" data-canonical-src="https://static.scarf.sh/a.png?x-pxid=5f9b700f-ee94-4940-b005-e72def008ffa&amp;page=README.md" style="max-width: 100%;"></a></p>
